ParlaMint-PT_2023-09-22.u579	We will now vote on proposal 4, from the PSD, in the parts in which we add a new Annex I and an Annex II. [[Subject to the vote, votes against the PS and the CFP were rejected, votes against the PSD, the CH, the BE and the NAP and abstentions against the IL and the L.]] It was as follows: ANNEX I [ as referred to in Article 44. o -A ] Creation of an exceptional scheme for the provision of devolved or underused buildings Chapter I General Part Article 1. the Purpose This Act regulates the programme of cooperation between the State and local authorities for the use of inactive public real estate (Programme) and establishes a special procedure for the assignment of temporary use to municipalities, the parish with more than 10 000 inhabitants or inter-municipal entities of public property in the state domain and property in the private domain of the State and public institutions which are returned or underused. Article 2 the Objectives and content 1 — The Programme promotes collaboration between, on the one hand, state-owned public entities or managers of devolved or underused buildings and, on the other, municipalities, parishes with more than 10 000 inhabitants or intermunicipal entities (local public entities), with a view to the use and profitability of these buildings, the prevention of their degradation and the dynamization of the capillary management of the public heritage. 2 — Collaboration between public entities holding or managing real estate and local public entities may take place, inter alia, in the following ways: (a) Accomplishment of public buildings returned or underused in the territory of the local public entity; (b) Support of the local public entity in the administrative, registral or matricial regularisation of buildings; (c) Support of the local public entity in the process of alienation or costly transfer of property to third parties; (d) Interventions for the preservation or rehabilitation of buildings by the local public entity; (e) Temporary assignment of use of the property to the local public entity for the realization of projects of public interest. Article 3 the real estate covered 1 — This law covers the property of the public domain of the State and the property of the private domain of the State and of public institutions which are devolved or underused. 2 — For the purposes of this Act: (a) Returned property, any urban building or autonomous fraction that, having areas that can be used, is unoccupied; (b) Underused property, all urban building or autonomous fraction whose areas effectively used correspond to less than 1 / 4 of the useful areas available. 3 — For the purposes of this law, they relate to urban and mixed buildings. 4 — For the purposes of paragraph 2 ( a ), the absence of contracts in force with water, gas and electricity supply undertakings or the absence of invoicing relating to water, gas and electricity consumption shall be evidenced. Article 4 the subjective scope The Programme regulated by this Act covers the following: (a) Local public entities, which are municipalities, or parish with more than 10 000 inhabitants, or intermunicipal entities; (b) State public entities which are holders or managers of the buildings referred to in the preceding article, in particular bodies, services and entities of the direct and indirect administration of the State. Article 5. The principle of honour 1 — The forms of collaboration referred to in Article 2. it should involve some form of counterpart, either by the collaboration provided by the local public entity, or by the assignment of temporary use of the property. (b) the provision of services or incorporation of goods with a view to the use, profitability, conservation or rehabilitation of buildings; Article 6. The monitoring of Programme 1 — The members of the Government responsible for the treasure and local administration shall appoint a supervisor for the duration of the Programme. ( b) verify, by sampling and using the stock exchange of evaluators from the Directorate-General for Treasury and Finance ( DGTF ), the assessments of the buildings submitted by the tenderers as provided for in Chapter III ; 3 — The single prosecutor appointed from among the official auditors of accounts or companies of official auditors of accounts entered in their list of the Order of Official Accountants and the Securities Market Commission. Chapter II Cooperation for the return on assets Article 7. the articulation of cooperation by the Directorate-General for Treasury and Finance 1 — Local public entities may, upon request or with the agreement of DGTF referred to in Article 8 ( 2 ). the provision of any of the services referred to in Article 2 ( a) and ( d ). o 2 — Any public entity holding property referred to in Article 3. it may request the cooperation of local public bodies under the Programme regulated by this Act and shall simultaneously inform DGTF of the request. Article 8. the lifting of devolved or under-used public buildings 1 — The local public entity may draw up and submit to DGTF a survey of the devolved or under-used public buildings located in its territory. 2 — The survey referred to in the previous paragraph shall be drawn up in a form made available by DGTF on its website. 3 — The local public authority which shall issue a public property collection valid and completed in accordance with the terms set out in the DGTF form shall receive as a counterpart credits for the temporary transfer of public property to be contracted in accordance with Chapter III, in the following proportion according to the number of inhabitants: (a) ≤ 10 000 inhabitants, the legitimate right of use on one property for every three buildings identified in the survey and validated by DGTF ; (b) > 10 000 and ≤ 50 000 inhabitants, the legitimate right of use on one property for every five buildings identified in the survey and validated by DGTF ; (c) > 50 000 inhabitants, the positive right of use on one property for every ten buildings identified in the survey and validated by DGTF. 4 — The power duty referred to in the previous paragraph shall be increased by 100 % if the buildings identified in the survey are classified. Article 9. the support for the administrative, record or matrix adjustment of buildings 1 — The DGTF or the entity holding the property, with the knowledge of that property, may request the support of the local public entity in the material and legal acts necessary for the regularization of the property in terms of urban licensing, horizontal property constitution, registration in the residential register, matrix registration or batching operation. 2 — As opposed to the support service provided for in the previous paragraph, the local public authority may receive: (a) appropriations for the temporary transfer of public buildings to be contracted under Chapter III; (b) a cash payment in accordance with the emolumentary table, approved by office of the members of the Government responsible for the areas of treasure and local administration, within 90 days of the publication of this law and after the hearing of the National Association of Portuguese Municipalities and the National Association of Freguesias. 3 — The determination of the counterparty of the local public body shall be made in the order of DGTF referred to in Article 7 ( 3 ). the 4 — In the order referred to in the previous paragraph, DGTF may confer on the local public entity the powers of representation of the property holder for the practice of the acts necessary for administrative, register or matrix regularisation. Article 10. Support in the transfer or transfer process from the property to third parties 1 — The DGTF or the entity holding the property, with the knowledge of that property, may request the local public authority: (a) Operational support in the promotion of profitable operations of the returned or under-used public property, including its placing on the market and the identification of potential stakeholders, in strict compliance with applicable public procurement rules; (b) receiving the property in temporary transfer for subcession or lease to third parties. 2 — As opposed to the activities provided for in the previous paragraph, the local public body may receive any of the counterparties referred to in paragraph 2 of the preceding Article. Article 11. the maintenance and preservation of buildings by the local entity 1 — The DGTF or the entity holding the property, with the knowledge of the property, may contract with the local public entity: (b) a percentage of the product to be received by the holder in the event of profitability of the property; 3 — In the case of rehabilitation operations carried out under this Act, the local public entity may promote and subscribe applications for allowances and support to which the project is eligible. Chapter III Special procedure for the assignment of temporary use Article 12. the Initiation of proceedings The procedure laid down in this Chapter shall commence with the submission of a proposal for a concession of use by the local public authority, which in this Chapter shall be designated as « proposer ». Article 13. The proposal for a concession for use 1 — The tenderer may submit to the DGTF a proposal for a concession for the use of immovable property which is devolved or under-utilised, with a view to bringing them to an end corresponding to the tasks pursued by him. 2 — The tenderer is always the assignee of the property and the first responsible for fulfilling the legal and contractual obligations relating to it. 3 — The proposal to be submitted by the tenderer shall include: (a) a project for the use of the property as provided for in the following Article; (b) a proposal for the duration of the assignment of use; (c) an assessment of the property in accordance with Article 15. ( d) a proposal for a consideration in accordance with Article 23. (e) the demonstration of the financial capacity of the proposer appropriate to the implementation of the project. Article 14. the project of use of the property 1 — The project of use of the property identifies the activity or end of public interest to which the Proponent intends to target the use of the property, paying attention to its location, the rules of the municipal instrument of management of the applicable territory and possible classification of the property. 2 — The project also includes a descriptive memory of any works of preservation or rehabilitation of the property and changes that the proposer intends to carry out in the property. 3 — The tenderer may involve public or private partners in the implementation of the project, and in this case shall include in the proposal the general terms of such cooperation. 4 — The agreement provided for in the previous paragraph does not confer any rights on the assignor to any partners and automatically expires with the termination of the assignment. 5 — The choice of a private partner by the tenderer respects the rules of public procurement. Article 15. the valuation of the property 1 — The proposal submitted by the proposer is accompanied by an assessment of the property carried out by the evaluator expert registered at the CMM, at the expense of the tenderer. 2 — The Single Prosecutor provided for in Article 6. the verification, by sampling and using the scholarship of assessors of the DGTF, the evaluations presented by the proposers. Article 16. the Instruction 1 — Within 15 days of receipt of the proposal, DGTF shall promote the hearing: (a) of the ministry protecting the entity or service to which the property is affected; (b) of the owner of the property; (c) of the entity responsible for the classification, in the case of the property being classified. 2 — The entities referred to in the previous paragraph shall deliver their opinion on the proposal within 45 days of the notification by DGTF. 3 — DGTF shall immediately forward to the tenderer a copy of the opinions provided for in this Article. 4 — DGTF must listen to the tenderer before making the final decision, informing him, in particular, on the likely meaning of the final decision. 5 — In the light of the opinions or draft decisions referred to in the previous paragraphs, the proposer may recast his proposal. Article 17. Decision 1 — DGTF shall decide to accept or reject the proposal within 90 days of its receipt. 2 — In the event of a reformulation of the proposal pursuant to paragraph 5 of the preceding article, the DGTF shall have a period of 45 days to decide, extending the period of the previous number if necessary. 3 — The tacit deferral of the proposal is made if, at the end of the period laid down for the decision, the proposal is not given and notified to the tenderer. 4 — The maximum head of the public legal person owned by the property or, in the absence thereof, the member of the Government responsible for the financial area shall deliver to the tenderer the property contained in the proposal submitted by it within 60 days of the express or tacit deferral of the proposal. 5 — In the event of non-compliance with the provisions of the previous paragraph, the urban policy instruments referred to in Article 54 shall apply. the following and legal regime of urban rehabilitation, approved by Decree-Law No 307 / 2009, of 23 October. 6 — The DGTF may only reject the proposal with the following grounds: (a) the illegality of the purposes or activities of the proposed use project; (b) the illegality or manifest disappropriation to the public interest of the works for the preservation or rehabilitation of the property provided for in the project; (c) the lack of demonstration of the financial capacity for the implementation of the project and its sustainability; (d) the manifest injury to the public interest in the acceptance of the counter-responsibility proposed by the tenderer, weighted the probability of alternatives to the profitability of the property; (e) the owner of the property has an alternative project for the use of the project, the execution of which is shown to start within six months ; (f) the DGTF or the owner of the property have a firm offer for the alienation or costly assignment of that which is generating revenue above that would result from the acceptance of the proposal of the tenderer. 7 — If the basis for the rejection of the proposal is one of the points (e) and (f) of the previous paragraph and the alternative operation does not take place within six months of the decision of DGTF, the tenderer shall have the right to request the re-evaluation of the proposal without the same grounds for rejection being invoked. 8 — the act of disregarding the proposal shall be made available to the member of the Government responsible for the treasury area. Article 18. the Legal Representative without prejudice to the hearing referred to in Article 16. the and of any rights to the consideration incurred by the tenderer, DGTF acts as legal representative of the owner of the property in the decision-making acts of the proposal and others that are necessary in the management of the buildings. Article 19. Form 1 — The favourable, express or silent decision of DGTF is quite a title for the tenderer to assume the use of the property and initiate the execution of the project. 2 — If the order of the DGTF is subject to condition, the contract shall form with the acceptance expressed by the tenderer of the conditions laid down in it. Article 20. the Expiry for non-implementation of project 1 — If the tenderer does not initiate the project within six months of the order of DGTF, the temporary assignment shall expire. 2 — On a reasoned request from the tenderer, DGTF may extend the time limit provided for in the previous paragraph. Article 21 the constitution of duties or charges The tenderer may not constitute on the property burden or charges, except by prior and express authorization from DGTF. Article 22. the works of preservation and rehabilitation of the property The acceptance by DGTF of the proposal implies the authorization to the proposer to carry out the works of conservation, rehabilitation or modification of the property that consists of descriptive memory integrated in the project. Article 23. the Financial Scheme of Allocation 1 — The temporary transfer of the property is in accordance with the principle of the burden enshrined in Article 54. that of Decree-Law No 280 / 2007 of 7 August, considering the assessment provided for in Article 15. the 2 — Counterfeiting of the tenderer may be carried out in one or more of the following ways: (a) the conduct by the tenderer of investments in conservation or rehabilitation works and which are incorporated into the property; (b) payment of income; (c) the delivery to the State of part of the revenue obtained with the exploitation of the property. 3 — In the event that the transfer is made solely by making investment in the property, the period of assignment shall be fixed according to the relationship between the current value of the property provided for in the assessment referred to in Article 15. the and the volume of investment in work to be carried out, plus a maximum period of three years to carry out the work. (b) 70 % for the owner of the property after the value of the investment is amortised. Article 24. the term of assignment of use 1 — The transfer of property shall be the maximum duration of 50 years. 2 — The DGTF may determine a period of less than the duration of the assignment proposed by the tenderer, on the basis of a relevant drawback to the public interest of the duration proposed by the tenderer and in the extent of the period determined by it for the depreciation of the investment made by the tenderer. 3 — The advance resolution of the concession by DGTF implies communication to the tenderer in advance of at least 60 days per year missing for the end of the contract and compensation for the investment made and not yet amortised. Chapter IV Final provisions Article 25. This Act shall enter into force on the first day of the second month following its publication. Article 26. the Subsidiary Regime In everything that is not specifically regulated in this Act, the provisions of the public real estate scheme, approved by Decree-Law No 280/2007 of 7 August 2007 shall apply in its current wording. ANNEX II ( referred to in Article 41 ). o-B) Transitional income allowance scheme to be allocated to less-income tenants Article 1. the object and scope The present law establishes an income allowance scheme to be allocated to tenants belonging to family aggregates with lower incomes, with lease contracts for housing. Article 2 Definitions 1 — For the purposes of this Act: (a) « Family aggregate » shall be regarded as the whole of persons constituted by the tenant and persons who, in accordance with the provisions of Article 13 (4) and (5). that of the IRS Code, with the necessary adaptations, integrate it. ( b) « RAB » means gross annual income as defined in Article 3. that of Decree-Law No 156 / 2015 of 10 August; ( c) « RABC », the corrected annual gross income defined in accordance with Article 4. ( d) « Return » means the monthly value of the remuneration due by the landlord for the enjoyment of housing; ( e ) « RMNA » means the annual national minimum remuneration in accordance with the New Urban Rent Scheme approved by Law 6 / 2006 of 27 February in its current wording; ( f ) « Effort rate » means the percentage of the RABC for the payment of the income of the respective year. 2 — For the purposes of point (a) of the previous paragraph, where the tenant does not reside in the place, temporarily or permanently, for reasons of illness or detention in social support establishments or equivalents, the total number of persons referred to in the previous numbers remaining on the leased place shall be considered as family aggregated by the tenant. 3 — The family aggregate, the RNA and other relevant elements for the determination of the RABC are related to the calendar year prior to the act to which the RABC declaration is directed, without prejudice to the fact that, in the case of this one being necessary to prove the RABC earlier, the information may be reported to the different calendar year. Chapter I Income allowance Section I General provisions Article 3. the Access Requirements 1 — You are entitled to the award of income allowance under this Act for leases with rental contracts for housing purposes, duly registered with the Tax Authority, which constitute their permanent housing and which meet the following requirements: (a) Affix income which implies that the RABC of the respective family aggregate is taxed up to 6. (b) have an effort rate equal to or greater than 33 % of RABC; (d) be more than 18 years on the date of conclusion of the lease contract. 2 — It is also a condition for the allocation of the allowance that: (a) the value of the income is within the maximum income values provided for in Article 10 ( a ). that of Decree-Law No 68 / 2019, of 22 May, and of Portarias No 176 / 2019, of 22 May, and 176 / 2019, of 6 June. (b) the type and area of the lease covered by the contract referred to in the above number are appropriate in relation to the size of the family aggregate. Article 4 Exclusions 1 — There is no place for the allocation of income allowance where any of the following situations occur: (a) It is not possible, on grounds of charge to the tenant or elements of his or her family aggregate, to access the information necessary for the calculation of his or her RABC, in particular where the necessary authorisation is not granted for access to his or her tax data or for the processing of pensions; ( b) The tenant shall not be in the place of his or her permanent residence, without prejudice to Article 2 ( 2 ). (c) the renter shall rent or lease the renter in any capacity, in whole or in part, even if authorised by the owner for that purpose; 2 — In the case of point (d) of the previous paragraph, the tenant shall demonstrate that the property is not suitable for satisfying the housing purpose of the family aggregate or is not in minimum living conditions, preferably by means of a document issued by the competent public services according to the territory and subject matter, in particular municipal services. Article 5. the requirement and procedure for the award of grant 1 — The tenant requires, at the Social Security Institute, I.P. ( ISS, I.P. ) or the municipality of the rental area, in the cases provided for in paragraph 7, the allocation of the income allowance, and the application must be properly instructed with the required documents and necessary for its assessment. 2 — The model of the application, its form of delivery, the necessary instructional documents and the procedures relating to the receipt, analysis and evaluation of applications shall be established by the authorities of the Government responsible for the areas of housing and social security, taking into account in particular the provisions of Article 28. o -A of Decree-Law No 135 / 99 of 22 April, in its current wording. 3 — The application for an income allowance may be submitted up to three months after notification of the lease contract to the Tax Administration or within three months prior to the termination or renewal of the contract. 4 — Social security services shall establish the procedure for each application for the award of the income allowance and, within 15 days of the date of submission of the income allowance or, if not properly instructed, of the date of delivery of the last of the elements necessary for its instruction, shall send to ISS, I.P. the relevant information for final decision-making of the application. 5 — ISS, I.P. decide on the application for income allowance within 15 days from the date of dispatch of the application by the social security services. 6 — The decision on the application for an income allowance shall be notified to the applicant. 7 — ISS, I.P. may conclude agreements with municipalities for the delegation of powers provided for in this Act in accordance with Law No 50 / 2018 of 16 August by transferring to them their respective funds. 8 — The municipalities may also decide to supplement the amount of the income allowance, based on their own resources, by communicating it to ISS, I.P. , to which they shall transfer the respective amount. Article 6. the decision of application 1 — Without prejudice to other facts determining the indifference in legal terms, there is a failure to grant income in the following situations: (a) any of the conditions of access referred to in Article 5 shall not be proved. ( b) There is a cause of exclusion in accordance with Article 4. the 2 — The application shall be granted in accordance with the limits of the allowance to be allocated as defined in Article 11. the 3 — The deferral of the application for income allowance shall take effect from the first day of the month following the end of the period for the decision of the application for income benefit, if the income updated at that date is due, or on the first day of the month on which it is due. Article 7. the lease allowance The lease allowance is financial support, granted to the tenant in the form of a non-refundable monthly grant, relating to the amount of income and intended to support him to maintain his permanent residence in the lease. Article 8. the amount of the lease allowance 1 — The amount of the lease allowance corresponds to 25 % of the income value, limited to the maximum income values calculated in accordance with Article 10 ( 1 ). the Decree-Law No 68 / 2019, of 22 May, and of Portaria No 176 / 2019, of 6 June. 2 — When the tenant is under 35 years of age, the income allowance is increased by 8, 34 percentage points as a percentage of the income value, while the tenant does not exceed that age limit. Article 9. the duration of the lease allowance 1 — The lease allowance is granted for a period of up to 24 months, renewable for equal and successive periods. 2 — The renewal provided for in the previous paragraph is required by the tenant and depends only on the maintenance of the assumptions of the allocation of the allowance and the non-occurrence of any cause of extinction. 3 — The first payment of the lease allowance shall be due from the date on which the decision of the application takes effect in accordance with Article 6 (3) and (4). the or, in case of renewal, from the first day of the month following the end of the previous period. Article 10. the maintenance of the right to lease allowance in force 1 — The death of the tenant to whom the grant for lease in force has been assigned shall not prejudice the maintenance of the right to such benefit by the person to whom the lease is transferred in accordance with Articles 57. o is 58. the Law No 6 / 2006 of 27 February, as amended by Laws Nos 31 / 2012 of 14 August and 79 / 2014 of 19 December, provided that the transmitter meets the conditions for the maintenance of the grant. 2 — For the purposes of the previous paragraph, the consignee shall notify the occurrence and require the maintenance of the income allowance to social security services in the rented housing area within a maximum of 60 days from the date of the death of the tenant under the expiry penalty of the right to benefit. 3 — The decision on the application for maintenance of the allowance shall take effect from the month following the date of the death of the tenant. Article 11. the termination of the lease allowance The lease allowance shall cease before the end of any of the duration periods referred to in Article 9. where: (a) the lease contract for which the benefit is paid is terminated; (b) the expiry of the entitlement to the death allowance of the tenant shall be verified without the possibility of a person entitled to the maintenance of the allowance in accordance with the preceding article or the period of time set out in this Act for compulsory communications; (c) the conditions for the award of the allowance provided for in Article 3. Stop checking. Section II Non-compliance and supervision Article 12. the non-compliance by the beneficiary of the income allowance with any obligations relating to access and maintenance of the allowance, in particular the failure to provide legally required evidence required by the ISS, I.P. , within the time limit set for that purpose, or the breach of the contract under which the grant is granted, determines the revocation or cancellation of the award decision, with due legal consequences. Article 13. the False Declarations The provision of false statements under the administrative procedure concerning the award of the allowance is punishable, in general terms of the criminal law. Article 14. The ISS, I.P, is responsible for monitoring. the monitoring of compliance with the provisions of this Act by the beneficiaries of the benefit. Article 15. management and cooperation between public entities 1 — It is up to ISS, I.P. the management of the allocation of income allowances, including analysis and decision on applications for the award and renewal of the grant or modification of its modality, and communications with the other public services in this field preferably made through electronic communication. 2 — Within and for the purposes of the provisions of the previous paragraph, the ISS, I.P. it promotes linkage with the competent public authorities and services to confirm the conditions on which the allocation, maintenance or modification of the income allowance depends, and may access information from the tax administration and the pension processing entities relevant to that purpose, in particular to verify whether the RABC of the leaser's family aggregate is greater than 6. the IRS step. 3 — Access to and exchange of information, in particular confirmation and information on the data referred to in the previous paragraph, shall be made through the use of the information media, always ensuring the protection of the data concerned. Chapter II Final and transitional provisions Article 16. the charges 1 — It is the State's responsibility, through the ISS, I.P. , to ensure the management and granting of the allowances provided for in this Act, by means of budgetary allocation to be entered annually in the respective budget. 2 — The funds necessary for the monthly payment of the financial support provided for in this Act shall be transferred monthly by the Directorate-General for Treasury and Finance for the account to be indicated by ISS, I.P. , at the request of that entity, which shall carry out transfers of the funds corresponding to the said financial support to the bank account identified by the beneficiaries until the 8th day of the month on which it relates. Article 17. access to data The allocation, renewal and maintenance of the income allowance depends on the authorization of the applicant and members of the family aggregate, for access by the ISS, I.P. the information of the tax administration and pension processors that is relevant for the purpose of awarding the benefit. Article 18. the duration of the benefit and evaluation of results 1 — Without prejudice to the provisions of the following paragraph, the income allowance may be applied for until the end of 2026. 2 — After four years of the entry into force of this Law, the conditions of the rental market for housing purposes and the impact of this scheme should be assessed in order to reassess their need and scope. Article 19. the replacement of the Porta 65 1 programme — The start of the income allowance provided for in this diploma replaces the Porta 65 programme, regulated by Decree-Law No 308 / 2007 of 3 September, amended by Decree-Law No 61 -A / 2008 of 28 March, by Decree-Law No 43 / 2010 of 30 April, by Law No 87 / 2017 of 18 August, by Law No 114 / 2017 of 29 December, and by Decree-Law No 90 - C / 2022 of 30 December. 2 — The support granted under Gate 65 shall, on 1 January 2024, become the income allowance provided for in this diploma, keeping the beneficiaries the support in accordance with existing conditions and conditions. Article 20. Entry into force 1 — This law shall enter into force on the first day of the month following its publication. 2 — Income allowance may be required and allocated from the date of entry into force of the State Budget Act for 2024.

[[Subject to the vote, votes against the PS, the CFP and the BE were rejected, votes against the PSD, the CH and the NAP and abstentions against the IL and the L.]] It was as follows: ANNEX III ( referred to in Article 41 ). o-C ) Chapter I General provisions Article 1. the object This Act approves exceptional measures to mitigate the impact of aggravating interest on housing by simplifying debt rescheduling as well as changing variable and fixed rate arrangements for credit contracts. Article 2 the scope This Act applies to credit contracts for the acquisition or construction of permanent own housing, covered by Decree-Law No 74 -A / 2017 of 23 June with variable interest rates, concluded with credit institutions, financial corporations and branches of credit institutions and financial institutions to be operated in Portugal. Article 3 the fees and emoluments The acts resulting from the application of this diploma shall be exempt from the payment of fees and emoluments, in particular in respect of the registration of the premises. Article 4 o Duty of information The entities referred to in Article 2. they advertise on their websites, instead of highlighting, and on bank statements the possibility of customers unilaterally require debt rescheduling, due to the rise of interest rates, under this law. Chapter II Debt rescheduling Article 5. the conditions for rescheduling of debt 1 — borrowers with credit contracts covered by this Act may request debt rescheduling in part of the benefit increases during the period in force of this measure resulting from the positive change in the reference interest rate underlying the contract. 2 — For the purposes of the previous paragraph, rescheduling operations shall allow the value corresponding to the increase in interest benefits to be redistributed for the time at which the reduction in the rate of effort or the main benefits occurs, providing the borrower with a fixed monthly benefit during the period of application of the measure. 3 — In any event, in particular if the reduction in the rate of effort or the main benefits does not occur, the debt securities must be paid in the last five years of the repayment period of the principal loan, without prejudice to the fact that, at the request of the borrower, the remaining amount may be paid, at the end of this period, in an additional final benefit. 4 — Debt securities subject to rescheduling may only be capitalised, with reference to the time when they are due, at the Euribor rate applicable to the main contract or, if the public guarantee provided for in the following Article is applied, at the interest rate implied in the public debt of the Republic disclosed by the Treasury and Public Debt Management Agency - IGCP, E.P.E. 5 — The rescheduling of debt pursuant to this Article shall not give rise to any: (a) contractual default; (b) activation of early maturity clauses; (d) ineffectiveness or termination of guarantees, including the effectiveness and validity of insurance, bail or leases; and (e) worsening of the contractual spread. 6 — In addition to the provisions in the previous paragraph, the rescheduling of debt cannot result in any specific registration or marking of customers as restructurings or risk clients due to that change in the Bank of Portugal's Credit Responsibilities Centre or through similar internal control mechanisms used by institutions referred to in Article 2. Article 6. the public guarantee 1 — Debt securities subject to rescheduling may benefit from personal guarantees provided by the State and by other public legal persons, within the maximum limits for the granting of personal guarantees provided for in the State Budget Act, 2 — Debts whose payment is made through the additional provision referred to in paragraph 3 of the preceding article may be eligible to cover part or all of that value, depending, in particular, on the period and amount of the debt subject to rescheduling. 3 — The Government shall, by resolution of the Council of Ministers, lay down the general rules and conditions for granting the guarantees provided for in this Article. 4 — The availability of public guarantees remains until 31 December 2024, subject to assessment and possible extension in the previous quarter. Chapter III Change of variable and fixed rate schemes Article 7. the change in variable and fixed rates of credit contracts for permanent own housing 1 — The entities referred to in Article 2. it is obliged to offer its customers an alternative credit to housing in the form of a fixed rate or benefit, either in the new contracts or in the contracts in force by means of a contractual change not subject to payment of any commission, penalties, taxes, fees or other forms of taxation, or to any form of internal or external marking of the borrower. 2 — Up to the end of 2024 the entities referred to in the previous paragraph report quarterly to the Banco de Portugal and to the Competition Authority information on: 3 — Within 30 days of the entry into force of this Act, the Banco de Portugal shall approve the model of the reporting forms provided for in the previous paragraph. 4 — Without prejudice to the preservation of banking and commercial secrecy and its other regulatory, prudential and behavioural supervisory and competition control competences, the Banco de Portugal and the Competition Authority shall publish their autonomous evaluations six-monthly on the evolution and basis of banking practices in relation to the evolution of the use of fixed and variable interest rates in Portugal. Chapter IV Final provisions Article 8. the supervision The Banco de Portugal shall monitor compliance with the provisions of this Law and, where appropriate, the application of its fines and ancillary penalties. Article 9. the Sanctions Scheme 1 — Failure to fulfil the obligations laid down in this Act or in its rules constitutes a punishable counter-ordination pursuant to Article 210. the General Scheme of Credit Institutions and Financial Societies ( RGICSF), approved by Decree-Law No 298 / 92 of 31 December, in its current wording. 2 — The violation of Article 3 ( 6 ). the financial institution constitutes a punishable counter-order practice in accordance with Article 211. the RGICSF. Article 10. This law shall enter into force on the day following its publication.
